- **ruflo-neural-trader**: FEDERATED - the claims inside are not verified by this repo. Nine skills that wrap the `neural-trader` npm engine inside a 71k-star agent harness: walk-forward backtests, Monte-Carlo and parameter sweeps run on the vendor's cloud, LSTM/Transformer/N-BEATS training, PageRank feature attribution, mean-variance optimisation by conjugate gradient, regime detection and VaR/CVaR.
